# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 019 DERIVATIVE COUNTRY PACKAGE EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# Owns the isolated setup, family reinforcement, focus-route lifecycle,
# district projects, ghost population decline, defeat handling, and annex
# cleanup for zombie, ghost, and golem derivative countries. These effects do
# not grant infantry_spawn_participant; derivative identity excludes their
# materialization from global Event 019 formation and lot history.
# ============================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Setup and private ledger initialization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_derivative_initialize_private_ledger = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_private_ledger_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_private_ledger_initialized
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_destroyed_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_count_generation = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_anomalous_request_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_actions = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integrated_districts = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_war_wins = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_depot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on a scenario or natural-release actor whose exact private ledger
# was built and proved before public derivative identity is installed. Both
# claimant and family actors adopt that ledger in place; clearing it here would
# orphan the actor's live army.
infantry_spawn_derivative_adopt_scenario_claimant_ledger = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_private_ledger_initialized
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_actions = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integrated_districts = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_war_wins = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_depot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_add_starting_ideas = {
	add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host
	add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts
	add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_disputed_command
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_fragmented_command
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_unstable_manifestation
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_broken_pattern
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Removes the ordinary Event 19 management, claimant, evolution,
# and Muster Board surfaces while preserving the derivative's exact private
# formation/claimant ledgers, their scheduled pulse and compaction markers,
# and scenario handoff flags. This is intentionally narrower than the ordinary-
# country finalizer, which would also erase the dynamic-breakaway transaction
# that still owns this country.
infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = {
	infantry_spawn_remove_ordinary_country_runtime_ideas_and_missions = yes
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_claimant_quartermaster_sovereign
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_claimant_field_prophet
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_claimant_barracks_tribune

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public identity, leaders, and route cosmetics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_derivative_classify_origin_region = {
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id
	if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = europe } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.europe }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = middle_east } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.middle_east }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = africa } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.africa }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = asia } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.asia }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = australia } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.australia }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = north_america } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.north_america }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { is_on_continent = south_america } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.south_america }
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Combines the stored identity and frozen origin-region tokens
# into one of the 91 reviewed cosmetic tags. There is deliberately no generic
# identity fallback: incomplete identity or geography fails closed.
infantry_spawn_derivative_apply_cosmetic_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.invalid }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.golem_species compare = less_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.invalid }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_region_id value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_region.south_america compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_cosmetic_tag = INFANTRY_SPAWN_[IDENTITY]_[REGION] }
			IDENTITY = "[This.GetInfantrySpawnDerivativeIdentityToken]"
			REGION = "[This.GetInfantrySpawnDerivativeRegionToken]"
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_set_base_cosmetic_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.claimant_breakaway }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.zombie_base }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.ghost_base }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.golem_base }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.invalid }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_apply_cosmetic_identity = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_set_claimant_cosmetic_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.claimant_breakaway }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.zombie_claimant }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.ghost_claimant }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.golem_claimant }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity_id = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_cosmetic_identity.invalid }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_apply_cosmetic_identity = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_find_claimant_character = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
		every_unit_leader = {
			limit = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
					CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid|.0]"
				}
				is_army_leader = yes
				is_female = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_promote_claimant_character = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_find_claimant_character = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character_found > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		retire_country_leader = yes
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype.quartermaster_sovereign compare = equals } }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character = {
				add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = oligarchism expire = "1965.1.1.1" } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype.field_prophet compare = equals } }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism elections_allowed = no }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character = {
				add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = fascism_ideology expire = "1965.1.1.1" } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype.barracks_tribune compare = equals } }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character = {
				add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = conservatism expire = "1965.1.1.1" } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype.iron_saint compare = equals } }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = no }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character = {
				add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = marxism expire = "1965.1.1.1" } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character = {
				add_country_leader_role = { promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = despotism expire = "1965.1.1.1" } }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_create_family_commander = {
	retire_country_leader = yes
	set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_host_commander_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_zombie_host_commander
			female = no
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_host_commander_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_ghost_host_commander
			female = no
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_master_builder_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_golem_master_builder
			female = no
			ideology = despotism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_create_family_council = {
	retire_country_leader = yes
	set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = no }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_host_council_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_zombie_host_council
			female = no
			ideology = marxism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_host_council_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_ghost_host_council
			female = no
			ideology = marxism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
		create_country_leader = {
			name = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_pattern_council_name
			picture = GFX_portrait_infantry_spawn_golem_pattern_council
			female = no
			ideology = marxism
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_leadership_installed
	}
}

# Initial-provider adapter. Claimant, zombie, ghost, and golem setup effects call
# this only after the provider-agnostic private package has initialized. A future
# provider installs its own public identity inside event19_setup_derivative and
# does not require a branch in this adapter.
infantry_spawn_derivative_install_starting_public_identity = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_set_base_cosmetic_identity = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_promote_claimant_character = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway } }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_create_family_commander = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_setup_derivative_identity_common = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none compare = equals }
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_scenario_dynamic_breakaway
							has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_scenario_dynamic_breakaway
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
					}
					infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
					infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num
						value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero
						compare = greater_than
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num
						value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero
						compare = greater_than
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num
						value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero
						compare = greater_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete
		infantry_spawn_initialize_global_system = yes
		infantry_spawn_abort_prototype_maintenance_trial = yes
		remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_preserved_prototype_burden
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_event19
		var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = {
			infantry_spawn_achievement_mark_claimant_or_derivative_revolt = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_unregister_all_evolution_memberships = yes
		infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_automatic_generation_suppressed
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_active
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_decisions_open
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_participant
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_scenario_dynamic_breakaway
					}
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
				}
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_adopt_scenario_claimant_ledger = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_audit_active
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_private_ledger_initialized
			infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_state_markers = yes
			infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_private_ledgers = yes
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_route = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_route.none }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine.none }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_initialize_private_ledger = yes
		infantry_spawn_derivative_add_starting_ideas = yes
		load_focus_tree = {
			tree = infantry_spawn_derivative_focus_tree
			keep_completed = no
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = neutrality
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_classify_origin_region = yes
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.opening_crisis_days }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_active
		activate_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_survive_former_parent_front
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_scenario_dynamic_breakaway } }
			infantry_spawn_schedule_country_pulse = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_setup_claimant_breakaway_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative
		infantry_spawn_setup_derivative_identity_common = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_install_starting_public_identity = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_setup_zombie_derivative_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id compare = equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative
		infantry_spawn_setup_derivative_identity_common = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_install_starting_public_identity = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_release_report = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_setup_ghost_derivative_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = constant:event19_ghost_family.family_id compare = equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative
		infantry_spawn_setup_derivative_identity_common = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_install_starting_public_identity = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_release_report = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.interval_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_days }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_setup_golem_derivative_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = constant:event19_golem_family.family_id compare = equals }
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative
		infantry_spawn_setup_derivative_identity_common = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_install_starting_public_identity = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_release_report = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# Country scope. Each nonhuman derivative receives exactly one family report
# after the complete identity package exists. Claimant breakaways deliberately
# bypass these reports and retain their claimant presentation.
infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_release_report = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_report_dispatched }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_report_dispatched
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.910 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.912 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.914 }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Natural registry-driven derivative revolt preflight
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# These helpers freeze one release mode, select either a claimant or the
# strongest locally present family, build a connected non-capital region from
# the selected formation origins, and freeze the exact Event 19 unit rows. The
# release path below implements the approved recreate/prove/delete substitute
# for the engine's missing exact division-subset transfer effect.

# Parent-country scope. Ordinary Evolution III claimant releases derive every
# selection value from the chosen claimant and its exact active unit rows. No
# family row, provider callback, or Evolution IV state is consulted.
infantry_spawn_select_natural_ordinary_claimant_release_evidence = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_pressure = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_influence = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_influence = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.untracked compare = greater_than }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out compare = less_than }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_evidence_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_build_natural_derivative_revolt_region = {
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_eligible_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_base_is_safe = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_state_is_safe = yes }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_eligible_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_eligible_states > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_eligible_state_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_eligible_states }
			infantry_spawn_calculate_selected_state_target = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_division_count }
			divide_temp_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target
				value = constant:infantry_spawn_family_pressure.revolt_units_per_state
				if_zero = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one
			}
			round_temp_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target
			clamp_temp_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target
				min = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one
				max = infantry_spawn_selected_state_target
			}

			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index
						value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant }
								check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
							}
							AND = {
								NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant } }
								check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
								OR = {
									NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
									check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
								}
							}
						}
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.untracked compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out compare = less_than }
					}
					var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_state_is_safe = yes
								NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states value = THIS } }
							}
							add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states value = THIS }
						}
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}

			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
				infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
					var:infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_state_is_safe = yes
							}
							if = {
								limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states value = THIS } } }
								add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states value = THIS }
							}
							add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS }
							save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
							save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state
						}
					}
				}
			}
			# If the claimant headquarters is the protected parent capital or is
			# otherwise ineligible, anchor on an exact loyal formation origin and
			# use that state as the breakaway headquarters. Family breaches use the
			# same origin-first rule without creating a claimant.
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states^num > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				}
				random_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_state_is_safe = yes
						is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states value = THIS }
					}
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS }
					save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
						save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state
					}
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target
						value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num }
			}

			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num
						value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_expansion_found = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				random_owned_state = {
					limit = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_state_is_candidate = yes }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_expansion_found = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_expansion_found = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_state_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_region_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_preferred_revolt_states
}

					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }

				infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes

				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_quality = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_coherence = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_combat_count = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_support_count = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_mobility_mismatch = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_expected_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_scope_identity_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_strength_is_safe = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }

				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_incoherence_score = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_heavy_supply_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_extreme_supply_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_live_finite_risk_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }

}

# Parent-country scope. Freezes every obligation row owned by the exact unit
# set. The unit UID is the authority; generation and lot links must agree with
# that unit's already-frozen identity before any state or division changes.
infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_obligation_set = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index compare = equals } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_scan_index }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_unit_match_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index } }
				}
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_row_indexes value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_generation_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles value = infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_issued_amounts value = infantry_spawn_obligation_issued_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_paid_amounts value = infantry_spawn_obligation_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_salvageable_paid_amounts value = infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts value = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values value = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
				add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_statuses value = infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_obligation_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_row_indexes^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_generation_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_unit_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_statuses^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_revolt_transaction = {
	# Fail closed before candidate lookup so a rejected invocation cannot inherit
	# a successful temporary result from another country in the same effect chain.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant } }
		infantry_spawn_select_natural_ordinary_claimant_release_evidence = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_natural_claimant_release_base_is_safe = yes
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_division_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
			}
			infantry_spawn_build_natural_derivative_revolt_region = yes
			infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_transfer_set = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.anomalous_claimant } }
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_required_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
			infantry_spawn_select_natural_derivative_revolt_candidate = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_required_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_required_claimant_uid }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
				infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_influence = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
					infantry_spawn_build_natural_derivative_revolt_region = yes
					infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_transfer_set = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
		infantry_spawn_select_natural_derivative_revolt_candidate = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_natural_family_dynamic_release_base_is_safe = yes
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
			}
			infantry_spawn_build_natural_derivative_revolt_region = yes
			infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_transfer_set = yes
		}
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. The frozen aligned arrays remain the authority for the
# whole transaction. Staging changes only recorded ownership state; it neither
# destroys a division nor writes an Event 19 progression counter.
infantry_spawn_stage_natural_derivative_source_set = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index
				value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_row = {
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_previous_unit_status = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_stage_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. Used only before the destination proof commits. Every
# source status and previous-status value returns to its frozen preflight value.
infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_source_set = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index
				value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_row = {
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_previous_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_restore_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
}

# Parent-country scope. State transfer is exact, connected, non-capital, and
# troop-neutral. The actor target is already frozen by create_dynamic_country.
infantry_spawn_transfer_natural_derivative_region_to_actor = {
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states
				value = THIS
			}
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
			transfer_state = PREV
			add_state_core = PREV
		}
	}
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
		set_capital = {
			state = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state
			remember_old_capital = no
		}
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. Copies only generation summary rows referenced by the
# frozen transfer set. Unit and lot counts are recalculated for the private
# subset rather than inheriting unrelated parent formations.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_rows = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_generation_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_generation_copy_index
				value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^num
				compare = less_than
			}
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_generation_copy_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_uid }
			infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_created_date = infantry_spawn_generation_created_date_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_stage = infantry_spawn_generation_evolution_stage_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_profile = infantry_spawn_generation_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_eligible_states = infantry_spawn_generation_eligible_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_selected_states = infantry_spawn_generation_selected_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_status = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_unit_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_unit_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_unit_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_unit_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_uid compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_unit_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_unit_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_scan }
				event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
					infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_uid compare = equals }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_matches = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_lot_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_matches }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_created_date_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_created_date }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_evolution_stage_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_stage }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_profile_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_profile }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_eligible_state_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_eligible_states }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_selected_state_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_selected_states }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_lot_count }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_unit_count }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_status }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_status < constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.resolved } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_generation_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num }
}

# Actor-country scope. Copies exact lot metadata but starts private unit and
# obligation accounting at zero; recreated units and copied obligations rebuild
# those counts from the frozen subset only.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_rows = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_lot_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_lot_copy_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_lot_copy_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_uid }
			infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_template_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_family_id = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				var:infantry_spawn_lot_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_origin_state }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_experience = infantry_spawn_lot_start_experience_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_equipment = infantry_spawn_lot_start_equipment_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_manpower = infantry_spawn_lot_start_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_command_owner = infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_status = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_previous_status = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_supply = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_training = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_equipment_state = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_prototype_disposition = infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_incident_count = infantry_spawn_lot_incident_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_claimant_susceptibility = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_susceptibility_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_demobilization_resistance = infantry_spawn_lot_demobilization_resistance_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_origin_state
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_origin_state }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_template_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_family_id }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_profile }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_origin_state_entries value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_origin_state }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_quality }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_coherence }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_start_experience_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_experience }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_start_equipment_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_equipment }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_start_manpower_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_start_manpower }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_command_owner }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_status }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_previous_status }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries value = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries value = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries value = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_supply_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_supply }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_training_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_training }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_equipment_state_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_equipment_state }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_prototype_disposition_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_prototype_disposition }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_claimant_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_combat_count }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_support_count }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_mobility_mismatch }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_incident_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_incident_count }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_susceptibility_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_claimant_susceptibility }
