			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_lot_demobilization_resistance_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_demobilization_resistance }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_selected_state_generation_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_generation_uid }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_selected_state_scope_entries value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_origin_state }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_selected_state_lot_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_uid }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_lot_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. The standardized national template is the one legitimate
# case whose engine name is not derived from its UID. Its components still come
# exclusively from the frozen source component ledger and consolidated registry.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_create_canonical_engine_template_from_manifest = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.zero }
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			division_template = {
				name = "National Muster Standard"
				is_locked = yes
				force_allow_recruiting = no
				regiments = { [UNIT_TOKEN] = { x = [COLUMN] y = [ROW] } }
			}
		}
		UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
		COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
		ROW = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_row|.0]"
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids^num compare = less_than } }
		infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_manifest_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_slot_kind value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_slot_kind.combat compare = equals } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { add_units_to_division_template = { template_name = "National Muster Standard" regiments = { [UNIT_TOKEN] = [COLUMN] } } }
				UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
				COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = { add_units_to_division_template = { template_name = "National Muster Standard" support = { [UNIT_TOKEN] = [COLUMN] } } }
				UNIT_TOKEN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token.GetTokenKey]"
				COLUMN = "[?infantry_spawn_unit_registry_assigned_column|.0]"
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_index = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_registry_cardinality.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_verify_current_engine_template = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_template = "National Muster Standard" }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes }
}

# Actor-country scope. Rebuilds an ordinary recorded template from the parent's
# exact component ledger through the existing consolidated static-token registry.
# This helper never adds a registry row or infers a template from a display name.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_current_ordinary_template = {
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_selection = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_combat_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_support_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_token = infantry_spawn_template_component_unit_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_slot = infantry_spawn_template_component_slot_kind_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_column = infantry_spawn_template_component_column_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_row = infantry_spawn_template_component_row_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_row = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				while_loop_effect = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_row < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_scan value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_token_ids^num compare = less_than }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_token_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_token compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_scan }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_row > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_registry_token_row }
					infantry_spawn_unit_registry_load_current_row = yes
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_row_indices value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_row_index }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_token_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_token_id }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_static_tokens value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_static_token }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_provider_ids value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_provider_id }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_slot_kinds value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_slot }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_groups value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_group }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_columns value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_column }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_rows value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_row }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_equipment_profiles value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_equipment_profile }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_quality_weights value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_quality_weight }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_compatibility_classes value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_compatibility }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_mobility_classes value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_mobility }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_supply_burdens value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_supply_burden }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_finite_risks value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_finite_risk }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_primary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_primary }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_secondary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_secondary }
					add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_same_support_type_tertiary value = infantry_spawn_unit_registry_current_same_support_type_tertiary }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_slot = constant:infantry_spawn_component_kind.combat } }
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_combat_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					}
					else = { add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_support_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_combat_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_combat_count }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_support_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_support_count }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_manifest_slot_kinds^0 = constant:infantry_spawn_component_kind.combat }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_combat_count value = infantry_spawn_current_combat_count compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_support_count value = infantry_spawn_current_support_count compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_canonical_template_uid = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
			infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_create_canonical_engine_template_from_manifest = yes
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_unit_registry_create_engine_template_from_manifest = yes }
		infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_verify_current_engine_template = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			infantry_spawn_append_current_template_row = yes
			infantry_spawn_unit_registry_append_manifest_component_ledger = yes
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
}

# Actor-country scope. Registered-family templates are rebuilt through their
# already-registered Event 19 provider callback, then checked against the exact
# source component count and family identity.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_current_family_template = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_component_slot_kind_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_component_kind.combat }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_component_unit_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_current_family_id compare = equals }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_family_provider_id = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			chaos_unit_family_registry_is_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index value = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^num compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_current_family_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id compare = equals }
				infantry_spawn_current_registry_row_can_form_derivative = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_family_provider_id = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_family_provider_id > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count value = infantry_spawn_current_combat_count compare = equals }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_support_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_rows_before_build = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_build_template = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_family_provider_id|.0]"
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_rows_added = infantry_spawn_template_component_template_uid_entries^num }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_rows_added = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_rows_before_build }
		infantry_spawn_verify_current_template_exists = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_exists > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_component_rows_added value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_component_count compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_append_current_template_row = yes
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
}

# Actor-country scope. Loads exact template/lot metadata and chooses only the
# existing ordinary-token or registered-family reconstruction contract.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_rows = {
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_initialize_tables = yes
	chaos_unit_family_initialize_registry = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_copy_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_template_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_copy_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
			infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_status = infantry_spawn_template_status_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_recruitment_mode = infantry_spawn_template_recruitment_mode_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_locked = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_spawn_only = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_technology_locked = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_pretechnology = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_locked = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_spawn_only_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_spawn_only = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_technology_locked = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_pretechnology = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
			infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_current_ordinary_template = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_row_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid }
			infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_experience = infantry_spawn_lot_start_experience_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment = infantry_spawn_lot_start_equipment_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower = infantry_spawn_lot_start_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none } }
				infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_current_family_template = yes
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_current_ordinary_template = yes }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_locked > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_spawn_only > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_spawn_only_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_technology_locked > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_pretechnology > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_template_copy_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	infantry_spawn_unit_registry_reset_selection = yes
}

# Actor-country scope. Recreates the exact claimant profile under the same UID,
# copies the claimant row without incrementing appearance totals, and binds the
# headquarters to the exact transferred headquarters state.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_claimant_identity = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_profile = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_name_variant = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_influence = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand_date = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_loyal_lots = infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_accepted_demands = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_refused_demands = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid compare = equals }
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state
		}
		infantry_spawn_set_current_claimant_profile_metadata = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_archetype value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_archetype compare = equals } }
			infantry_spawn_create_current_claimant_commander = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_commander_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			every_unit_leader = {
				limit = {
					meta_trigger = {
						text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
						CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid|.0]"
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_commander_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_commander_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_profile }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_archetype }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_name_variant }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_influence }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand_date }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.revolted }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_loyal_lots }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_accepted_demands }
				add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_refused_demands }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_system_active
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_one_active
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_copy_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. Recreates exactly one frozen formation while preserving
# every recorded ledger identity and live-manifest field.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreate_current_unit = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_delete_cohort_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_template_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_family_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_experience = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_previous_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_claimant_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_inputs_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_family_id compare = equals }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_inputs_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid compare = equals }
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_unit_row = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_inputs_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state
			event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = { is_controlled_by = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_unit_rows_before_create = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_divisions_before_create = num_divisions }
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes
		every_country_division = { add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes value = THIS } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"Unbidden Formation [UNIT_UID]\" division_template = \"National Muster Standard\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [START_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [START_MANPOWER]" owner = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor count = 1 id = [DELETE_COHORT_ID] allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no } }
					UNIT_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid|.0]"
					START_EXPERIENCE = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_experience|.2]"
					START_EQUIPMENT = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment|.2]"
					START_MANPOWER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower|.2]"
					DELETE_COHORT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_delete_cohort_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"Unbidden Formation [UNIT_UID]\" division_template = \"Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [START_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [START_MANPOWER]" owner = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor count = 1 id = [DELETE_COHORT_ID] allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no } }
					UNIT_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid|.0]"
					TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_template_uid|.0]"
					START_EXPERIENCE = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_experience|.2]"
					START_EQUIPMENT = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_equipment|.2]"
					START_MANPOWER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_start_manpower|.2]"
					DELETE_COHORT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_delete_cohort_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_create_delta = num_divisions }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_create_delta = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_divisions_before_create }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_create_delta = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
			infantry_spawn_capture_and_mark_current_live_division = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_live_division NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_unit_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				infantry_spawn_append_current_unit_row = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_unit_rows_before_create compare = greater_than }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid compare = equals }
					}
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_status }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_previous_status }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_claimant_uid }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_spawn_province_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_spawn_provinces^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_transfer_eligible_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_transfer_eligible_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
					}
					event_target:infantry_spawn_current_live_division = {
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_status }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_previous_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_previous_status }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_unit_claimant_uid }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_distinct_group_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_distinct_group_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_distinct_equipment_profile_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_distinct_equipment_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_compatibility_mismatch_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_compatibility_mismatches^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_incoherence_score = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_incoherence_scores^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_heavy_supply_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_heavy_supply_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_extreme_supply_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_extreme_supply_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_finite_risk_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_finite_risk_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_project_equipment_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_project_equipment_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_technology_locked_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_technology_locked_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unresolved_variant_component_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_unresolved_variant_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_distinct_combat_component_types = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_distinct_combat_types^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_camelry = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_camelry^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_bicycle = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_bicycle^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_amphibious_tank = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_amphibious_tank^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_flame_element = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_flame_element^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_artillery = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_babel_has_engineers = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_babel_engineers^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_natural_transfer_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_pretechnology_presence^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						event_target:infantry_spawn_current_live_division = { set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_pretechnology_formation = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_pretechnology_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index } }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_pretechnology_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid } } }
							add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_template_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_template_uid }
						}
						set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_technology_locked_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_current_unit_uid }
						set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation
					}
					event_target:infantry_spawn_current_live_division = {
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_infantry_weapons^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_infantry_weapons = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_motorised_infantry^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_motorised_infantry = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_mechanised_infantry^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_mechanised_infantry = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_armored_car^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_armored_car = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_artillery = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_rocket_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_rocket_artillery = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_anti_air^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_anti_air = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_anti_tank^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_anti_tank = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_light_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_light_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_medium_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_medium_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_heavy_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_modern_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_modern_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_mechanized^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_mechanized = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_helicopter^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_helicopter = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_land_cruiser^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_land_cruiser = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_artillery = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_armor = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
					}
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
}

infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreate_frozen_units = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreate_current_unit = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. Copies the frozen obligation rows verbatim without
# allocating a new global UID or registering a generated formation. Private
# debt/manpower totals and per-lot balances are rebuilt from those exact rows.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_obligation_rows = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_obligation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_obligation_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_generation_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_lot_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_resource_profile_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_issued_amount_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_issued_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_paid_amount_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_paid_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_salvageable_paid_amount_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_salvageable_paid_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope, before public derivative setup. The provisional actor is
# marked as a derivative before any row appender runs, so the private rebuild
# cannot increment ordinary Event 19 formation or lot history.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_provisional_actor = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_automatic_generation_suppressed
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_mode = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.anomalous_claimant } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id }
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
	}
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid }
	infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_private_ledgers = yes
	infantry_spawn_clear_ordinary_country_auxiliary_arrays = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_private_ledger_initialized
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
	infantry_spawn_derivative_initialize_private_ledger = yes
	infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_generation_rows = yes
	infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_lot_rows = yes
	infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_template_rows = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_claimant_identity = yes
	}
	infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreate_frozen_units = yes
	infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_copy_obligation_rows = yes
	infantry_spawn_validate_all_ledgers = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. The recreate/prove/delete path is identity-neutral: it
# consumes no allocator and writes no Event 19 lifetime/progression total. The
# snapshots below turn that contract into a runtime transaction gate.
infantry_spawn_snapshot_natural_derivative_global_accounting = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_generation_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_generation_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_lot_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_lot_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_template_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_unit_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_unit_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_obligation_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_obligation_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_claimant_uid = global.infantry_spawn_next_claimant_uid }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_delete_cohort_id = global.infantry_spawn_next_delete_cohort_id }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_generation = global.infantry_spawn_generation }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_current_evolution_stage = global.infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }

infantry_spawn_verify_natural_derivative_global_accounting = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_generation_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_lot_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_template_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_unit_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_obligation_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_obligation_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_claimant_uid compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_delete_cohort_id compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_generation value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_generation compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_current_evolution_stage compare = equals } }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date compare = equals } }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count compare = equals } }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count_present = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_formation_count
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_formation_count }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_formation_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count compare = equals } }
					}
				}
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_formations value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_formations compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_lots value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_lots compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_destroyed_formations value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_destroyed_formations compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_demobilized_lots value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_demobilized_lots compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_integrated_lots value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_integrated_lots compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_lot_incidents value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_lot_incidents compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_management_successes value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_management_successes compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_management_failures value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_management_failures compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_requests value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_requests compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_crises value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_crises compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_failed_coups value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_failed_coups compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_takeovers value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_takeovers compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_revolts value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_revolts compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}
