			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_heavy_armor } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_heavy_armor } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_modern_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_modern_armor } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_modern_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_modern_armor } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_armor } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_armor } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_mechanized^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_mechanized } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_amphibious_mechanized^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_amphibious_mechanized } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_helicopter^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_helicopter } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_helicopter^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_helicopter } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_land_cruiser^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_land_cruiser } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_land_cruiser^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_land_cruiser } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_artillery } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_artillery } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_self_propelled_super_heavy_artillery } } }
			OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_armor } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_live_gate_super_heavy_armor^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_gate_super_heavy_armor } } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_actor_division_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. This is the destructive transaction gate. Every frozen
# UID must resolve to one and only one live replacement, the actor may contain
# no other division, and every copied accounting row must close against the
# exact frozen subset before the parent is allowed to destroy a source scope.
infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_provisional_actor = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_expects_actor_marker = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_build_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				NOT = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
				NOT = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country }
				NOT = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_division_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_template_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_lot_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_mode }
				NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_mode value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce compare = equals } }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first compare = equals } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first compare = equals } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_owned_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_selected_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_controlled_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_core_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	every_owned_state = {
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_owned_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_selected_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			if = {
				limit = { is_controlled_by = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_controlled_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_core_of = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_core_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_parent_selected_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country = {
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_parent_selected_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_capital
	add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_capital value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_anchor_state }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_capital_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	capital_scope = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_capital value = THIS } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_capital_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_owned_state_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_selected_state_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_controlled_state_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_core_state_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals } }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_parent_selected_state_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_capital_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_capital

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_marked_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_natural_transfer_claimant_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_natural_transfer_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
			is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_marked_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_marked_division_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = equals } } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_requires_recreated_strength_ceiling = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_uid compare = equals }
			}
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_division
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_division
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_division = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_actor_division_matches_frozen_index = yes }
			clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_origin
			clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_live_scope
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_origin value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_origin_states^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_division = { add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_live_scope value = THIS } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_scope_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_origin value = THIS } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_expected_live_scope value = THIS } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_scope_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_actor_division_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_origin_bound > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_live_scope_bound > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }

					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_spawn_province_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_spawn_provinces^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_family_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_experience_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index compare = equals }

				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_expected_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_lot_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_proof_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_identity_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_created_date_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_created_date compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_evolution_stage_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_stage compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_profile compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_eligible_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_eligible_states compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_selected_state_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_selected_states compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_expected_lots compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_expected_units compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_generation_status compare = equals }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_generation_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_leader_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_unit_leader = {
			limit = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
					CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid|.0]"
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_leader_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_headquarters_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_fields_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_expected_claimant_headquarters
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_expected_claimant_headquarters value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_profile compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_archetype compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_name_variant compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_influence compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_demand_date compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.revolted compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_loyal_lots compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_accepted_demands compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_claimant_refused_demands compare = equals }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_fields_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			var:infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_expected_claimant_headquarters value = THIS } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_headquarters_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_leader_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_headquarters_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_claimant_fields_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_expected_claimant_headquarters
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}
