# Parent-country scope. Recreates one missing frozen source UID using its exact
# original template name and delete cohort, then rebinds the existing source
# row instead of appending a duplicate. A still-live row is simply rebound and
# normalized; duplicate live UIDs fail the recovery proof.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recover_current_parent_unit = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_uid compare = equals }
		}
		save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_division
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_uid }
	infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index }
	infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_template_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index }
	infantry_spawn_find_template_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count < constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.second }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_lot_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state
					event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_divisions_before_create = num_divisions }
				clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes
				every_country_division = { add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes value = THIS } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_template_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_template_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
					event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = {
						meta_effect = {
							text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"Unbidden Formation [UNIT_UID]\" division_template = \"National Muster Standard\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [START_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [START_MANPOWER]" owner = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country count = 1 id = [DELETE_COHORT_ID] allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no } }
							UNIT_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.0]"
							START_EXPERIENCE = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							START_EQUIPMENT = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							START_MANPOWER = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							DELETE_COHORT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.0]"
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					event_target:infantry_spawn_current_origin_state = {
						meta_effect = {
							text = { create_unit = { division = "name = \"Unbidden Formation [UNIT_UID]\" division_template = \"Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]\" start_experience_factor = [START_EXPERIENCE] start_equipment_factor = [START_EQUIPMENT] start_manpower_factor = [START_MANPOWER]" owner = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country count = 1 id = [DELETE_COHORT_ID] allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no } }
							UNIT_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.0]"
							TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.0]"
							START_EXPERIENCE = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							START_EQUIPMENT = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							START_MANPOWER = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.2]"
							DELETE_COHORT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index|.0]"
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_create_delta = num_divisions }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_create_delta = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_divisions_before_create }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_create_delta = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_new_scope_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					every_country_division = {
						limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes value = THIS } } }
						save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_division
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_new_scope_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_new_scope_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						# create_unit cannot recover the deleted division's unexposed live
						# composition. A completed source recreation permanently fail-closes
						# another natural recreate/delete transaction for this parent.
						set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_recreation_consumed
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
						add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_recreated_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_uid }
					}
					else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_precreate_division_scopes
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_division
			}
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_division = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_apply_frozen_parent_live_identity = yes }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_live_division }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
}

# Parent-country scope. This proof is required after every rollback and after
# partial-delete recovery. It verifies the full frozen UID/cohort set, exact
# source row/live-scope binding, and all auxiliary membership before unlocking.
infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_parent_source_set = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_expects_actor_marker = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_max_recreated_strength = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_requires_recreated_strength_ceiling = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_recreated_unit_uids value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_proof_requires_recreated_strength_ceiling = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid compare = equals }
			}
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_division
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_unit_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_division
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_profile = infantry_spawn_lot_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_quality = infantry_spawn_lot_quality_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_coherence = infantry_spawn_lot_coherence_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_combat_count = infantry_spawn_lot_combat_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_support_count = infantry_spawn_lot_support_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_lot_mobility_mismatch = infantry_spawn_lot_mobility_mismatch_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_division = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_actor_division_matches_frozen_index = yes }
			clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_origin
			clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_live_scope
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_origin value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_origin_states^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_division = { add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_live_scope value = THIS } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_scope_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = {
				if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_origin value = THIS } } set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index = {
				if = { limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_live_scope value = THIS } } set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_scope_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_current_actor_division_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_origin_bound > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_live_scope_bound > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_spawn_province_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_spawn_provinces^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_family_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_experience_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_equipment_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_manpower^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_unit_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_claimant_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index compare = equals }
					OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_transfer_eligible_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_transfer_eligible_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_transfer_eligible_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_transfer_eligible_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } } }
					OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_technology_locked_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_technology_locked_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } } }
					OR = { AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_pretechnology_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } AND = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_pretechnology_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_uid } } } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_row_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_origin
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_expected_live_scope
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope. Deletes only the frozen globally unique Event 19 cohort
# IDs with no refund. This deliberately avoids destroy_unit so rollback cannot
# masquerade as a combat loss or feed death/casualty progression. The final
# zero-division proof prevents annexation from transferring or hiding a unit.
infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_remove_and_prove_destination_replacements = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^num compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_cohort = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_exact_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_cohort compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_exact_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_exact_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
			delete_unit = { id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_cohort disband = no }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_exact_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { num_divisions = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_global_accounting = {
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_generation_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_lot_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_template_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_template_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_unit_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_obligation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_obligation_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_claimant_uid }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_delete_cohort_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_delete_cohort_id }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_generation = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_generation }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_current_evolution_stage }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_next_evolution_check_date }
	}
	else = { clear_variable = global.infantry_spawn_next_evolution_check_date }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_country_count }
	}
	else = { clear_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_country_count }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count_present > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_formation_count = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_last_generation_formation_count }
	}
	else = { clear_variable = global.infantry_spawn_last_generation_formation_count }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_formations = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_formations }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_lots = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_lots }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_destroyed_formations = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_destroyed_formations }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_demobilized_lots = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_demobilized_lots }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_integrated_lots = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_integrated_lots }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_lot_incidents = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_lot_incidents }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_successes = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_management_successes }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_management_failures = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_management_failures }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_requests = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_requests }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_crises = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_crises }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_failed_coups = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_failed_coups }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_takeovers = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_takeovers }
	set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_total_claimant_revolts = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_snapshot_total_claimant_revolts }
}

# Parent-country scope. Mandatory rollback order: prove the destination army is
# gone, return territory with troop transfer disabled, recreate only missing
# frozen source UIDs, restore their row/live bindings, then prove the complete
# source set before either transaction lock can clear.
infantry_spawn_recover_natural_derivative_revolt_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_complete = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_recreated_unit_uids
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { exists = yes }
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_remove_and_prove_destination_replacements = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_destination_cleanup_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
				every_owned_state = {
					if = { limit = { is_core_of = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor } remove_state_core = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_actor_core_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
				every_owned_state = {
					limit = { is_core_of = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_actor_core_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_actor_core_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				# The provisional actor is not a defeated public derivative. This
				# flag suppresses annex listeners only for this synchronous effect.
				set_global_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_annex_bypass
				annex_country = {
					target = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor
					transfer_troops = no
				}
				clr_global_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_annex_bypass
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_global_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_annex_bypass
							event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { exists = yes }
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				}
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovered_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovered_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovered_state_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals } } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recover_current_parent_unit = yes
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_parent_source_set = yes
		infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
		infantry_spawn_verify_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_complete = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. Deletes only the exact frozen globally unique source
# cohort IDs with no refund; no destroy_unit/death semantic is used.
# No ledger accounting is committed here. The complete zero-UID proof and
# exact total division delta must both pass; otherwise the mandatory recovery
# effect restores any source rows already destroyed in this loop.
infantry_spawn_delete_and_prove_natural_derivative_source_set = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_divisions_before_delete = num_divisions }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_cohort = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_cohort compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_row = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_cohort compare = equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
				delete_unit = { id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_cohort disband = no }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid compare = equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_uid_live_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_delta = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_divisions_before_delete }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_delta = num_divisions }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_delta value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = equals } } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Parent-country scope, after source-zero proof but before any accounting write.
# This closes every balance the commit will subtract so the commit phase cannot
# fail halfway through and require a lossy ledger reconstruction.
infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_division_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
				AND = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count < constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_row value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged compare = equals }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_debt = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_manpower = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_debt = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_manpower = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_uid compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_uid compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_manpower = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan }
				}
				else = { add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_debt = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_units compare = less_than } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_debt compare = less_than } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_manpower compare = less_than } }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_debt = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_debt }
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_manpower = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_manpower }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_empty_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_uid compare = equals } }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_scan }
			infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_uid compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_current_lot_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				while_loop_effect = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_scan compare = equals } }
						add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_current_lot_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_current_lot_units compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_empty_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_lot_scan = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_units compare = less_than } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_empty_lots compare = less_than } }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_generation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_equipment_debt value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_debt compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_manpower compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
	}
}

# Parent-country scope, after the complete source-zero and commit-readiness
# proofs. This freezes every field the deterministic accounting commit changes.
# A later post-proof compares the result against these exact expected values;
# the pre-commit recovery path is never called after these writes begin.

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_active_lots = infantry_spawn_active_lot_count }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_equipment_debt = infantry_spawn_equipment_debt }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_equipment_debt = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_commit_ready_total_debt }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
