			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_manpower = infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_manpower_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_manpower = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_transfer_manpower }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_status = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_previous_status = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_command_owner = infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_previous_status = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_status = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.transferred }
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_empty_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_status = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_command_owner = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_unit_counts value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_units }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_debts value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_debt }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_manpower value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_manpower }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_statuses value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_status }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_previous_statuses value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_previous_status }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_command_owners value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_command_owner }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_claimant_uids value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_expected_claimant_uid }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_lots = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_empty_lots }

	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_generation_expected_unit_counts
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_generation_expected_lot_counts
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_transfer_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_empty_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^num compare = less_than } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_uid compare = equals } }
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_transfer_units = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index }
				infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_unit_counts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					}
					add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_empty_lots = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_scan_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_units = infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_units = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_transfer_units }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_lots = infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_lots = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_empty_lots }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_generation_expected_unit_counts value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_units }
			add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_generation_expected_lot_counts value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_expected_lots }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_generation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_profile = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_name_variant = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_influence = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_demand = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_demand_date = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_accepted_demands = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_refused_demands = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_divisions < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_lots < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_equipment_debt < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_manpower_liability < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants < constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_expected_unit_counts^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_generation_expected_unit_counts^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_generation_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_rows^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_delete_cohorts^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_generation_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_lot_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_template_uids^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_statuses^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_previous_statuses^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_rows^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_statuses^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_previous_statuses^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_uids^num compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. Runs only after the destination proof and complete
# source-zero proof. Historical UID/cohort rows remain in place, transition to
# transferred states, and shed only the exact active/debt balances represented
# by the frozen subset. No global Event 19 total or allocator is written.
infantry_spawn_commit_natural_derivative_source_accounting = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transfer_staged compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_row = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out }
			subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
			infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
			infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_transfer_eligible_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation
	}
	else = { set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation
	}
	else = { set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row value = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index compare = equals }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_resource_profiles^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_equipment_family.manpower } }
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_amounts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index }

				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_outstanding_debt_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_obligation_outstanding_debt_values^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_amount_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_outstanding_debt_value_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_obligation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_row = constant:infantry_spawn_obligation_state.transferred }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_obligation_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_equipment_debt = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability < constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } } set_variable = { infantry_spawn_manpower_liability = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_index }
		infantry_spawn_find_lot_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_unit_count_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.transferred }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					}
					subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_lot_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_rows^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.claimant_controlled }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_lot_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_row = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_rows^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_cohort = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_delete_cohorts^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_template_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_previous_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_remaining_claimant_unit_previous_statuses^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_cohort compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_generation_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_lot_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_template_uid compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.claimant_loyal }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_previous_status compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
			}

					limit = {
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid

						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_previous_unit_status value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_previous_status compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
					}
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_unit_status = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_status }

				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_expected_status }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_remaining_unit_row = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }

		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }

		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { remove_unit_leader = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
				CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid|.0]"
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.revolted }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count > constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first }
			}
			infantry_spawn_clear_claimant_crisis_state = yes
			infantry_spawn_refresh_claimant_presence_flags = yes
			infantry_spawn_refresh_active_claimant_archetype_ideas = yes
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_history
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
	}
	infantry_spawn_validate_all_ledgers = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. Exact post-accounting proof for every field changed by
# infantry_spawn_commit_natural_derivative_source_accounting. A failure here is
# terminal and locked; the pre-commit annex/recreate recovery must not run over
# a partially committed source ledger.
infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_source_accounting_committed = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_num_divisions = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_divisions_before_delete }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_num_divisions = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_num_divisions compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_division_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_divisions compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_lot_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_lots compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_equipment_debt value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_equipment_debt compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_manpower_liability value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_manpower_liability compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants compare = equals } }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_history }
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_pending
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_revolt_warning_active
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_claimant_revolt_warning_index
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_claimant_revolt_due_date
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_row_indexes^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_cohort = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_delete_cohort_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index }
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_expected_origin
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_expected_origin value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_origin_states^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_expected_origin value = THIS } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_live_uid_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_live_cohort_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		every_country_division = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_uid compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_live_uid_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {

					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_delete_cohort_id

				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_live_cohort_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_uid compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_delete_cohort_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_cohort compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_generation_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_lot_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_template_uids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_spawn_province_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_spawn_provinces^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_family_ids^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_experience_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_experience^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_start_equipment_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_row value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_unit_start_equipment^infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_index compare = equals } }

					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_unit_origin_bound = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
