		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_expected_claimant_headquarters
		add_to_temp_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_expected_claimant_headquarters value = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_claimant_headquarters_state }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_profile compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_archetype compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_name_variant compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_influence compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_demand compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_demand_date compare = equals }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.revolted }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_accepted_demands compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_snapshot_claimant_refused_demands compare = equals }
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries^infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_expected_claimant_headquarters value = THIS } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_claimant_headquarters_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		every_unit_leader = {
			limit = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
					CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid|.0]"
				}
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_claimant_leader_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_expected_claimant_headquarters
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_claimant_headquarters_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_claimant_leader_count > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_pretechnology_unit_uid_entries^num > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_pretechnology_formation }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_technology_locked_unit_uid_entries^num > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_has_technology_locked_formation }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none }
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_system_active
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_one_active
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_two_active
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_three_active
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_system_active }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_one_active }
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_two_active
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_three_active
						NOT = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.second }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_system_active }
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_one_active
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_two_active }
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_three_active
						NOT = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_expected_active_claimants > constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.second }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_system_active }
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_one_active
						has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_two_active
						NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_three_active }
						NOT = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

# Actor-country scope after the irreversible source accounting proof. Public
# identity is installed while both sides remain locked, the copied commander is
# proved male, and the exact former-parent war is proved before unlock.
infantry_spawn_finalize_natural_derivative_revolt_actor = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_finalize_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant } }
		infantry_spawn_setup_claimant_breakaway_identity = yes
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_setup_derivative = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id|.0]"
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_active
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
			infantry_spawn_derivative_is_parent_isolated = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant }
					has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
					NOT = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.anomalous_claimant }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid compare = equals }
				}
			}
		}
		infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_provisional_actor = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_identity_proof = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_identity_proof = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_male_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			every_unit_leader = {
				limit = {
					meta_trigger = {
						text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
						CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid|.0]"
					}
					is_female = no
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_male_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_male_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_identity_proof = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_identity_proof > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction = yes
					is_faction_leader = no
				}
				leave_faction = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
					var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = { exists = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent }
				}
				declare_war_on = {
					target = var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
					var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = { exists = yes }
					has_war_with = var:infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_finalize_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Parent-country scope. Post-commit failure cannot be rolled back without
# rewriting committed historical accounting, so both countries fail closed.
infantry_spawn_lock_failed_natural_derivative_post_commit = {
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
	infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor
			event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { exists = yes }
		}
		event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
			infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

# Parent-country scope after a fully proved pre-commit restoration. Claimant
# releases resolve as the existing visible failed coup. A claimant-independent
# family breach remains contained for one pulse window, but loses control and
# adds persistent saturation pressure before its acknowledgement event.
infantry_spawn_apply_natural_derivative_precommit_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_gui_selected_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_family_deferred_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.natural_family_deferred_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_natural_family_breach_deferred days = infantry_spawn_natural_family_deferred_days }
		subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_muster_control = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.natural_family_deferred_control_loss }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_evolution_saturation_pressure = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.natural_family_deferred_pressure_gain }
		clamp_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_muster_control min = constant:infantry_spawn_control.minimum max = constant:infantry_spawn_control.maximum }
		infantry_spawn_recalculate_country_pressure = yes
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.206 }
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes }
			infantry_spawn_execute_selected_claimant_failed_coup = yes
		}
	}
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_mode
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_nonce
}

# Parent-country scope before any dynamic actor exists. Only staged source
# statuses can have changed, so exact restoration plus the complete source proof
# is sufficient; this path does not synthesize a country or choose a fallback.
infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_before_actor = {
	infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_source_set = yes
	infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_parent_source_set = yes
	infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
	infantry_spawn_verify_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		infantry_spawn_apply_natural_derivative_precommit_failure = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

# Parent-country scope for every actor-created failure before source accounting
# commit. The recovery effect owns the exact delete/annex/recreate/prove order.
infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_with_actor = {
	infantry_spawn_recover_natural_derivative_revolt_transaction = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_complete > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		infantry_spawn_apply_natural_derivative_precommit_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_allocate_natural_derivative_release_nonce = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.infantry_spawn_next_natural_release_nonce } }
		set_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_natural_release_nonce = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce = global.infantry_spawn_next_natural_release_nonce }
	add_to_variable = { global.infantry_spawn_next_natural_release_nonce = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_mode = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_nonce = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce }
}

# Parent-country scope. Implements the approved exact transfer transaction for
# every natural multi-state release mode. Replacements and their private ledger
# are fully proved before source deletion; accounting is committed only after a
# complete source-zero proof; public identity and war are the final locked step.
infantry_spawn_run_natural_derivative_exact_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	clear_temp_array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recovery_recreated_unit_uids
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country
	infantry_spawn_allocate_natural_derivative_release_nonce = yes
	infantry_spawn_prepare_natural_derivative_revolt_transaction = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		infantry_spawn_snapshot_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
		infantry_spawn_stage_natural_derivative_source_set = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_transfer_preflight_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
			create_dynamic_country = {
				original_tag = THIS
				save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_automatic_generation_suppressed
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_parent_country }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_mode = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant } }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none }
					clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.anomalous_claimant } }
					clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_uid }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_claimant_archetype }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
					clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id }
					clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
					clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype
				}
				else = {
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor
					event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { exists = yes }
				}
				infantry_spawn_transfer_natural_derivative_region_to_actor = yes
				event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
					infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_build_provisional_actor = yes
					infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_provisional_actor = yes
				}
				infantry_spawn_verify_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
					}
					infantry_spawn_delete_and_prove_natural_derivative_source_set = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_delete_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready = yes
						if = {
							limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_ready > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
							infantry_spawn_snapshot_natural_derivative_source_commit_accounting = yes
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_commit_snapshot_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
								infantry_spawn_commit_natural_derivative_source_accounting = yes
								infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_source_accounting_committed = yes
								if = {
									limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
									event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = { infantry_spawn_finalize_natural_derivative_revolt_actor = yes }
									infantry_spawn_restore_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
									infantry_spawn_verify_natural_derivative_global_accounting = yes
									infantry_spawn_prove_natural_derivative_source_accounting_committed = yes
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_owned_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_selected_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_controlled_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_core_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
									event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
										every_owned_state = {
											add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_owned_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
											if = {
												limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS } }
												add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_selected_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
												if = {
													limit = { is_controlled_by = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
													add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_controlled_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
												}
												if = {
													limit = { is_core_of = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor }
													add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_core_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
												}
											}
										}
									}
									set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_parent_selected_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
									every_owned_state = {
										limit = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states value = THIS } }
										add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_parent_selected_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
									}
									if = {
										limit = {
											check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor_finalize_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
											check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_global_accounting_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
											check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_source_post_commit_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
											check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_owned_states value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals }
											check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_selected_states value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals }
											check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_controlled_states value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals }
											check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_actor_core_states value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_revolt_states^num compare = equals }
											check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_final_parent_selected_states = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
										}
										event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_actor = {
											infantry_spawn_record_derivative_revolt_history = yes
											clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
											clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
										}
										clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
										clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
										clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_request_failed
										clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_mode
										clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_nonce
										set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
									}
									else = { infantry_spawn_lock_failed_natural_derivative_post_commit = yes }
								}
								else = { infantry_spawn_lock_failed_natural_derivative_post_commit = yes }
							}
							else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_with_actor = yes }
						}
						else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_with_actor = yes }
					}
					else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_with_actor = yes }
				}
				else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_with_actor = yes }
			}
			else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_before_actor = yes }
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_abort_natural_derivative_before_actor = yes }
	}
	else = {
		infantry_spawn_apply_natural_derivative_precommit_failure = yes
	}
}

# One-state countries cannot surrender a non-capital region. This proof instead
# requires that the whole live army is exactly the claimant-free selected
# family set, then allows the registered provider to take over the same tag.
infantry_spawn_prove_natural_family_same_tag_takeover = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_family_row_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_country
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id > constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.none } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
				num_of_controlled_states < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one
				num_of_controlled_states > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_family_breach_deferred
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = no
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.untracked compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_status_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index value = constant:infantry_spawn_unit_status.transferred_out compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_lot_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_lot_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_template_uid = infantry_spawn_unit_template_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			every_country_division = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_uid compare = equals }
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
			}
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_scope_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
						has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_claimant_uid
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_generated_identity = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_unit_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_generation_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_generation_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_lot_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_lot_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_template_uid compare = equals }
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_event19_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_scope_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_origin_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			var:infantry_spawn_unit_origin_state_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { is_controlled_by = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_country }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_origin_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unit_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_live_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_scope_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
						NOT = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_origin_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_family_row_count = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_index = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_family_row_count value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_division_count compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_family_row_count compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_execute_natural_family_same_tag_takeover = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero }
	infantry_spawn_prove_natural_family_same_tag_takeover = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_proof_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_rows_before = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_lot_rows_before = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_generation_rows_before = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_template_rows_before = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_divisions_before = num_divisions }
		infantry_spawn_allocate_natural_derivative_release_nonce = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent = event_target:infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_country }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_origin_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_origin_generation_uid }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_mode = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce }
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
		clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_parent_isolation_proved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_setup_derivative = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id|.0]"
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
		infantry_spawn_validate_all_ledgers = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_initialized
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_active
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_country
				infantry_spawn_derivative_is_parent_isolated = yes
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_recreated_transfer
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_family_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_provider_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_release_nonce value = infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_nonce compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_unit_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_unit_rows_before compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_lot_rows_before compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_generation_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_generation_rows_before compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_template_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_template_rows_before compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = num_divisions value = infantry_spawn_natural_same_tag_divisions_before compare = equals }
			}
			infantry_spawn_record_derivative_revolt_history = yes
			remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state
			remove_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_survive_former_parent_front
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_active
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_days
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_creation_in_progress
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_management_temporarily_locked
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_mode
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_natural_release_transaction_nonce
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_revolt_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_lock_failed_natural_derivative_post_commit = yes }
	}
	else = { infantry_spawn_apply_natural_derivative_precommit_failure = yes }
}

# Runs after claimant resolution on the same country pulse. Multi-state hosts
# use the exact actor transaction; the verified one-state case transforms the
# same tag only when its entire live army is the frozen claimant-free family.
infantry_spawn_begin_natural_independent_family_breach = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.independent_family }
	infantry_spawn_select_natural_derivative_revolt_candidate = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_transfer_attempted_this_pulse = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
		if = {
			limit = { num_of_controlled_states < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.two }
			infantry_spawn_execute_natural_family_same_tag_takeover = yes
		}
		else = { infantry_spawn_run_natural_derivative_exact_transaction = yes }
	}
}

# Claimant crises prefer a family-union release only when Evolution IV has an
# eligible derivative-capable family. Otherwise the same claimant proceeds by
# the ordinary Evolution III exact-record path, with no registry dependency.
infantry_spawn_begin_selected_claimant_natural_revolt = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_transfer_attempted_this_pulse = constant:infantry_spawn_weight_result.one }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant }
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_has_evolution_iv = yes
			chaos_unit_family_registry_is_aligned = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.anomalous_claimant }
		infantry_spawn_select_natural_derivative_revolt_candidate = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_selected_registry_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_natural_derivative_release_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_natural_release_mode.ordinary_claimant }
		}
	}
	infantry_spawn_run_natural_derivative_exact_transaction = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Registry lookup and exact one-formation materialization
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_derivative_find_registry_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
			chaos_unit_family_registry_is_aligned = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				check_variable = { var = chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id compare = equals }
					infantry_spawn_current_registry_row_can_form_derivative = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index = chaos_unit_family_registry_index }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Finds the immutable lifecycle owner for an existing
# derivative. Availability, spawning, and lot-policy changes may stop future
# formations, but must never strand defeat or final cleanup.
infantry_spawn_derivative_find_cleanup_registry_row = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
			chaos_unit_family_registry_is_aligned = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = constant:chaos_unit_family_registry.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index < constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
				check_variable = { var = chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^num compare = less_than }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^chaos_unit_family_registry_index value = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id compare = equals }
					infantry_spawn_current_registry_row_can_clean_derivative = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index = chaos_unit_family_registry_index }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_unit_family_registry_index = infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_family_id = global.chaos_unit_family_id_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id = global.chaos_unit_family_provider_id_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_select_reinforcement_origin = {
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_generation_country
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				infantry_spawn_state_is_eligible = yes
				has_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_state_is_eligible = yes
				has_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site
			}
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { infantry_spawn_state_is_eligible = yes } }
		capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state }
	}
	else = {
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_state_is_eligible = yes }
			save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_materialize_one_family_formation = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	# Failed preflight has not mutated anything, so its caller may refund. Once
	# publication begins, refunds stay locked until exact rollback proves that
	# every engine object, ledger row, auxiliary row, and aggregate was restored.
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_rollback_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	infantry_spawn_initialize_global_system = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_initialize_private_ledger = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_find_registry_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { infantry_spawn_contributes_to_ordinary_evolution_history = yes }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			infantry_spawn_country_has_eligible_state = yes
			infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_select_reinforcement_origin = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_origin_state }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_train_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			infantry_spawn_snapshot_management_request_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_rollback_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			infantry_spawn_allocate_generation_uid = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_stage.anomalous_muster }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_profile.anomalous }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_eligible_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_selected_state_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_unit_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_status = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.open }
			infantry_spawn_append_current_generation_row = yes
			infantry_spawn_prepare_current_state_context = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_lot_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_family.registered_anomalous }
			infantry_spawn_create_current_registered_lot = yes
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
				infantry_spawn_append_selected_state_row = yes
				meta_effect = {
					text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_spawn_unit = yes }
					PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id|.0]"
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_status = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.resolved }
				infantry_spawn_update_current_generation_totals = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
					infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_unit_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			}
			else = {
				infantry_spawn_rollback_management_request_transaction = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_rollback_valid > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_rollback_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_rally_claimant_guard = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_can_rally_claimant_guard = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_payment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_infantry_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_support_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_truck_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_train_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_fuel_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_request_paid_manpower_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			infantry_spawn_snapshot_management_request_transaction = yes
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			infantry_spawn_generate_selected_claimant_requested_lot = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_request_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_stage.command_fracture }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_profile = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_profile.command_fracture }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid }
				infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_evolution_stage_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = infantry_spawn_current_evolution_stage }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_profile_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = infantry_spawn_current_generation_profile }
				}
				else = { infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_status = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.resolved }
				infantry_spawn_update_current_generation_totals = yes
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
						subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
					}
					clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_audit_active
					clear_variable = infantry_spawn_country_stage
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_cost.another_formation_army_experience }
					multiply_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_xp_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.negative_one }
					add_army_experience = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_xp_cost
					add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_sustained
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.reinforcement_cooldown_days }
					set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				}
				else = {
					infantry_spawn_rollback_management_request_transaction = yes
					clear_variable = infantry_spawn_country_stage
				}
			}
			else = {
				infantry_spawn_rollback_management_request_transaction = yes
				clear_variable = infantry_spawn_country_stage
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_guard_succeeded < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_request_failed
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope on an initialized derivative. Event 19 still evaluates these
# countries, but uses their registered native family across the same smooth
# diminishing state-coverage curve. The private ledger retains exact identity;
# derivative identity excludes every append from ordinary-country history.
infantry_spawn_derivative_process_global_manifestation = {
	infantry_spawn_begin_country_generation = yes
	infantry_spawn_unregister_all_evolution_memberships = yes
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_participant
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index }
		while_loop_effect = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index value = infantry_spawn_lot_uid_entries^num compare = less_than } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = infantry_spawn_lot_generation_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index
						value = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_status_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.integrated }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_previous_status_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_lot_state.integrated }
				set_variable = { infantry_spawn_lot_command_owner_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_command_owner.state }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_lot_index = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_generation_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_generation_status_entries^infantry_spawn_generation_row_index = constant:infantry_spawn_generation_status.resolved }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
				subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_unresolved_generation_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			}
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_last_closed_generation_uid = infantry_spawn_current_generation_uid }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_count = infantry_spawn_current_generation_unit_count }
		}
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_audit_active
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_generation_stacked
	}
	# The shared generator deliberately supplies weighted coverage, exact lot
	# accounting, and provider dispatch. Remove only the ordinary country-facing
	# runtime it creates; the just-closed private ledger and family units remain.
	infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = yes
	infantry_spawn_schedule_country_pulse = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_count_live_family_divisions = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_live_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_family_id
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_family_id value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_family_id compare = equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_live_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_evaluate_current_family_management = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_count_live_family_divisions = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_find_registry_row = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_evaluate_management = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id|.0]"
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_pay_current_family_management = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_pay_management_action = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id|.0]"
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_refund_current_family_management = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_refund_management_action = yes }
			PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id|.0]"
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Provider-backed reinforcement and sustainment actions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_derivative_unlock_zombie_templates = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_trainable_family_ids value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id } } }
		add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_trainable_family_ids value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id }
	}
	every_country_division = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_family_id
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_event19_family_id value = constant:event19_zombie_family.family_id compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_template_uid = infantry_spawn_event19_template_uid }
		ROOT = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
					set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Unbidden Muster [TEMPLATE_UID]" is_locked = yes }
				}
				TEMPLATE_UID = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_template_uid|.0]"
			}
		}
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_training_authorized
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_authorize_zombie_training = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_evaluate_current_family_management = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_pay_current_family_management = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_unlock_zombie_templates = yes
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_rally_zombie_band = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_is_zombie = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_cooldown }
			check_variable = { var = manpower value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_manpower_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_infantry_equipment_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_manpower_cost }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_infantry_equipment_cost }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_materialize_one_family_formation = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_training_authorized }
				infantry_spawn_derivative_unlock_zombie_templates = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.reinforcement_cooldown_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_rollback_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			add_manpower = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_manpower_cost
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement.zombie_infantry_equipment_cost }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_manifest_paid_family_formation = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_evaluate_current_family_management = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_spawn > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_train > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.train_or_spawn }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_pay_current_family_management = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_materialize_one_family_formation = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.reinforcement_cooldown_days }
				set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_reinforcement_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_materialization_rollback_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
				infantry_spawn_derivative_refund_current_family_management = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_pay_family_sustainment = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_evaluate_current_family_management = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_can_sustain > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_management_action = constant:infantry_spawn_family_management_action.sustainment }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_pay_current_family_management = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_provider_payment_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_actions = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_sustained
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.sustainment_cooldown_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_begin_diplomacy_cooldown = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.diplomacy_cooldown_days }

}

# Expects infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope to be a validated
# neighboring ordinary country. The source-country array prevents repeat
# transfers or later submission demands against the same agreement partner.
infantry_spawn_derivative_register_diplomatic_agreement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_countries value = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope } }
		}
		add_to_array = { array = infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_countries value = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope }
	}
	diplomatic_relation = {
		country = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope
		relation = non_aggression_pact
		active = yes
	}
	add_opinion_modifier = { target = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope modifier = medium_increase }
	event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope = {
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = medium_increase }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_begin_diplomacy_cooldown = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_offer_zombie_containment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_can_offer_zombie_containment = yes
			FROM = { infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_diplomacy_target_is_valid = yes }
		}
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_containment_pp }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_containment_manpower }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_containment_equipment }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost }
		event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_scope = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_containment_equipment producer = ROOT }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_register_diplomatic_agreement = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_swap_family_burden_to_claimant = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_disputed_command }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_disputed_command add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_personal_staff }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_fragmented_command }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_fragmented_command add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_bound_to_commander }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_unstable_manifestation }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_unstable_manifestation add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_bound_procession }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_broken_pattern }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_broken_pattern add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_master_bound_constructs }
	}
}

	}
