# COUNTRY scope. Claimant appointment and replacement both create a character
# before the row can be proven. These helpers freeze and restore the entire
# parallel claimant ledger so a failed identity transaction cannot leave a
# free commander, an orphan row, or a false active-claimant count.
infantry_spawn_derivative_snapshot_claimant_transaction = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_active_count = infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_selected_index = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_selected_index = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_rollback_claimant_transaction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = { remove_unit_leader = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
			CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid|.0]"
		}
	}
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^num value = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count compare = greater_than } }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_archetype_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_name_variant_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_influence_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_demand_date_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_headquarters_state_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_loyal_lot_count_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_accepted_demand_count_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
		remove_from_array = { array = infantry_spawn_claimant_refused_demand_count_entries index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_row_count }
	}
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_active_count }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_snapshot_selected_index }
	infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = yes
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no }
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_prepare_claimant_route_identity = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_transaction_started = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_find_claimant_character = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_character_found > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_nonhuman_derivative
			infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count value = constant:infantry_spawn_event.maximum_claimants compare = less_than }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_snapshot_claimant_transaction = yes
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_transaction_started = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		infantry_spawn_select_current_claimant_profile = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_selected > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			infantry_spawn_allocate_claimant_uid = yes
			infantry_spawn_select_current_claimant_headquarters = yes
			infantry_spawn_create_current_claimant_commander = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_appointed_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
			every_unit_leader = {
				limit = {
					meta_trigger = {
						text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
						CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid|.0]"
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_appointed_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_appointed_character_found > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
					has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_headquarters
					infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				}
				infantry_spawn_append_current_claimant_row = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
						infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes
						check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid compare = equals }
					}
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.recognized }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid }
					set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_archetype }
					set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointed
					infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = yes
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_succeeded < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_transaction_started > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_rollback_claimant_transaction = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route_pending
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_claimant_route = {
	infantry_spawn_derivative_prepare_claimant_route_identity = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_appointment_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route_pending
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		if = {
			limit = {
				infantry_spawn_all_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
				infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			}
			clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
		}
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_route = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_route.claimant_sovereignty }
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_route
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_route
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route
		infantry_spawn_derivative_set_claimant_cosmetic_identity = yes
		infantry_spawn_derivative_promote_claimant_character = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_war_state }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_command_estates }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_swap_family_burden_to_claimant = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state }
			swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_decisive_war_plan }
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_collective_route = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_route = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_route.collective_host }
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_route
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_route
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_restrained
	infantry_spawn_derivative_set_collective_cosmetic_identity = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_create_family_council = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_command_host }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_district_network }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_swap_family_burden_to_collective = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_defensive_compact }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_species_route = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_route = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_route.species_command }
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_route
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_route
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_subordinated
	infantry_spawn_derivative_set_species_cosmetic_identity = yes
	infantry_spawn_derivative_create_family_commander = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_unrecognized_host add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_realm }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_family_domain }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_swap_family_burden_to_species = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state add_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_extinction_of_old_rule }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_logistics_idea_slot = {
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_seized_muster_districts
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_command_estates
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_district_network
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_family_domain
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_host_method
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_bands_method
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_captured_auxiliaries_method
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_network
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_sustainment_method
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_sustainment_method
	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_auxiliary_sustainment_method
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_refresh_logistics_idea_slot = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_family_sustainment_unlocked
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site_count > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
				has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_network
				has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_sustainment_method
				has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_sustainment_method
				has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_auxiliary_sustainment_method
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	}
	infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_logistics_idea_slot = yes
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_concentrated_doctrine = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_sustainment_method
		}
		else = { add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_host_method }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_scattered_doctrine = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_sustainment_method
		}
		else = { add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_bands_method }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_captured_auxiliaries_doctrine = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_auxiliary_sustainment_method
		}
		else = { add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_captured_auxiliaries_method }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_sustainment_idea_stage = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_network
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_concentrated_doctrine = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine.concentrated_host }
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_concentrated_doctrine
	infantry_spawn_derivative_refresh_logistics_idea_slot = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_scattered_doctrine = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine.scattered_bands }
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_doctrine
	infantry_spawn_derivative_refresh_logistics_idea_slot = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_captured_auxiliaries_doctrine = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_doctrine.captured_auxiliaries }
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_captured_auxiliaries_doctrine
	infantry_spawn_derivative_refresh_logistics_idea_slot = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_resolve_former_parent_pressure = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state } remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_pursued_by_former_state }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_decisive_war_plan } remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_decisive_war_plan }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_defensive_compact } remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_collective_defensive_compact }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_extinction_of_old_rule } remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_species_extinction_of_old_rule }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_regional_predator } }
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_outward_muster
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent_pressure_resolved
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_active
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_replace_claimant = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_transaction_started = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_has_claimant_route = yes
			infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure }
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid
			check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_lookup_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_uid }
		infantry_spawn_find_claimant_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_claimant_row_index }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_snapshot_claimant_transaction = yes
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_transaction_started = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid = constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_uid }
			infantry_spawn_select_current_claimant_profile = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_profile_selected > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
				infantry_spawn_allocate_claimant_uid = yes
				infantry_spawn_select_current_claimant_headquarters = yes
				infantry_spawn_create_current_claimant_commander = yes
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
				every_unit_leader = {
					limit = {
						meta_trigger = {
							text = { has_id = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
							CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid|.0]"
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_character_found = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_character_found > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
						has_event_target = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_headquarters
						infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
					}
					infantry_spawn_append_current_claimant_row = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes
							infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index_is_valid = yes
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_claimant_uid_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index value = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid compare = equals }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index }
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_uid }
						set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_current_claimant_archetype }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.recognized }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_uid }
						set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_archetype }
						clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
						infantry_spawn_derivative_promote_claimant_character = yes
						if = {
							limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure } }
							set_variable = { infantry_spawn_claimant_status_entries^infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_index = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_status.retired }
							if = {
								limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count > constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.none } }
								subtract_from_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_claimant_count = constant:infantry_spawn_claimant_count.first }
							}
							meta_effect = {
								text = { remove_unit_leader = [CLAIMANT_ID] }
								CLAIMANT_ID = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_uid|.0]"
							}
							set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_claimant_index }
							infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_parent_runtime_surface = yes
							clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_preserved
							set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_replaced
							set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_continuity_decided
							add_stability = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.stability_small
							add_army_experience = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.army_experience_small
							set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_succeeded < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_replacement_transaction_started > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_uid = infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_uid }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_archetype = infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_archetype }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_selected_claimant_index = infantry_spawn_derivative_replaced_claimant_index }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_rollback_claimant_transaction = yes
			if = {
				limit = { infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = yes }
				clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ledger_invariant_failure
				clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_processing_deferred
				clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
				infantry_spawn_derivative_promote_claimant_character = yes
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_identity_creation_failure
		if = {
			limit = { infantry_spawn_claimant_ledgers_are_aligned = no }
			infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_preserve_claimant = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_claimant_route = yes }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_preserved
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_replaced
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_continuity_decided
		add_stability = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.stability_small
		add_command_power = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.command_power_small
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# State projects, submission warning, and family decline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# Called from a state scope. The country keeps a bounded ledger of only the
# states on which this package writes markers, so final cleanup never scans the
# world and never touches another derivative country's markers.
infantry_spawn_derivative_register_current_marker_state = {
	save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_derivative_current_marker_state
	ROOT = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					is_in_array = {
						array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
						value = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_current_marker_state
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_array = {
				array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
				value = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_current_marker_state
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_secure_muster_depot = {
	FROM = {
		set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_muster_depot
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_register_current_marker_state = yes
		add_compliance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.compliance_gain
	}
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_depot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_establish_sustainment_site = {
	FROM = {
		set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_register_current_marker_state = yes
		add_compliance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.compliance_gain
	}
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_is_zombie = yes }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.infantry_equipment_small }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_is_ghost = yes }
		add_command_power = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.command_power_small
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_is_golem = yes }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.coal_golem_equipment_small }
	}
	add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
	infantry_spawn_derivative_refresh_logistics_idea_slot = yes
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_start_district_integration = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state = FROM }
	FROM = {
		set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_underway
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_register_current_marker_state = yes
	}
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_active
	activate_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_integrate_conquered_district_mission
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_start_zombie_district_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_can_start_zombie_district_integration = yes
			FROM = { infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_state_can_be_integrated = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_integration_pp }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_integration_manpower }
		add_manpower = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.zombie_integration_equipment }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.zombie_muster }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.zombie_integration_days }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_bands_numbered }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.zombie_numbered_integration_days }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_start_district_integration = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_start_ghost_district_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_can_start_ghost_district_integration = yes
			FROM = { infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_state_can_be_integrated = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.ghost_integration_pp }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.ghost_integration_cp }
		add_command_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.ghost_anchor }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.ghost_integration_days }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_procession_anchored }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.ghost_anchored_integration_days }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_start_district_integration = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_start_golem_district_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_can_start_golem_district_integration = yes
			FROM = { infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_state_can_be_integrated = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.golem_integration_pp }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.golem_integration_coal }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = coal_golem_equipment_1 amount = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost }
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.golem_integration_trains }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.golem_foundry }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.golem_integration_days }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_foundries_open }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.golem_foundry_integration_days }
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_start_district_integration = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_complete_district_integration = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_infrastructure_levels = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.golem_infrastructure_levels }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode
		}
		var:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
				}
				clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_underway
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = ROOT
					is_controlled_by = ROOT
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
					ROOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.zombie_muster compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.ghost_anchor compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.golem_foundry compare = equals }
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integrated_district
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.zombie_muster compare = equals } } }
					set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_muster_district
					set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_population_pressure
					add_compliance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.zombie_integration_compliance
					add_resistance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.zombie_integration_resistance
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.ghost_anchor compare = equals } } }
					set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_recognized_anchor
					add_compliance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.ghost_integration_compliance
					add_resistance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.ghost_integration_resistance
					ROOT = { set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_anchor_network_established }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode.golem_foundry compare = equals } } }
					set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_bound_district
					add_compliance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.golem_integration_compliance
					add_resistance = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_expansion.golem_integration_resistance
					meta_effect = {
						text = { add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = [LEVEL] instant_build = yes } }
						LEVEL = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_infrastructure_levels|.0]"
					}
				}
				ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_integrated_districts = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_active
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_cancel_district_integration = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state }
		var:infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
					check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
				}
				clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_underway
			}
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_active
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_state
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_days
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_mode
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_start_submission_warning = {
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target = FROM }
	set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.submission_warning_days }
	set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_warning_active
	activate_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_warning_mission
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_complete_submission_warning = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target }
		var:infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target = {
			if = {
				limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target_is_valid = yes }
				save_event_target_as = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target_scope
				ROOT = {
					set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_wargoal_expire_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_timer.postwar_recognition_days }
					meta_effect = {
						text = {
							create_wargoal = {
								type = annex_everything
								target = event_target:infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target_scope
								expire = [EXPIRE_DAYS]
							}
						}
						EXPIRE_DAYS = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_wargoal_expire_days|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_warning_active
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.submission_cooldown_days }
	set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_days
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_cancel_submission_warning = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_warning_active
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_target
	clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_days
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_apply_ghost_decline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_is_ghost = yes
			has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_cooldown }
			any_controlled_state = { impassable = no check_variable = { state_population_k > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { impassable = no check_variable = { state_population_k > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			set_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_register_current_marker_state = yes
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_percent = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.base_population_fraction }
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_anchor_network_established } }
				set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_percent = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.anchored_population_fraction }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_managed } }
				set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_percent = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.managed_population_fraction }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_factor = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.maximum_population_fraction }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_hard_max = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.hard_death_cap }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_exact_percent = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.infantry_spawn_ghost_decline }
			chaos_meter_register_state_civilian_deaths_percent = yes
			set_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_population_pressure
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline.interval_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_decline_days }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_suppress_fragmentation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			infantry_spawn_derivative_package_is_active = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_fragmentation_cooldown }
			NOT = { has_political_power < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.suppress_fragmentation_pp }
			check_variable = { var = army_experience value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.suppress_fragmentation_xp compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.suppress_fragmentation_pp }
		add_political_power = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_decision_cost.suppress_fragmentation_xp }
		add_army_experience = infantry_spawn_derivative_negative_cost
		add_stability = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.stability_medium
		add_command_power = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.command_power_medium
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_fragmentation_suppressed
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.fragmentation_cooldown_days }
		set_country_flag = { flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_fragmentation_cooldown days = infantry_spawn_derivative_cooldown_days }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Isolated country pulse, victory, defeat, and final annex cleanup
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

infantry_spawn_derivative_check_regional_predator = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_regional_predator_ambition
			infantry_spawn_derivative_hierarchy_is_consolidated = yes
			infantry_spawn_derivative_family_transformation_is_complete = yes
			infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainable_reinforcement_is_established = yes
			has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent_pressure_resolved
			infantry_spawn_derivative_has_regional_territorial_foothold = yes
			NOT = { num_of_controlled_states < constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.regional_predator_states }
			check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_war_wins value = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_runtime.regional_predator_wars compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_idea = infantry_spawn_derivative_regional_predator }
		}
		remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_outward_muster
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_regional_predator
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_run_country_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_package_is_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_route_pending
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_route = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_route.none }
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_choose_claimant_route = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_former_parent_pressure_resolved }
				NOT = { infantry_spawn_derivative_has_live_former_parent = yes }
			}
			infantry_spawn_derivative_resolve_former_parent_pressure = yes
		}
		infantry_spawn_derivative_apply_ghost_decline = yes
		infantry_spawn_derivative_check_regional_predator = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_record_war_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { infantry_spawn_derivative_package_is_active = yes }
		add_to_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_war_wins = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
		add_war_support = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_reward.war_support_small
		infantry_spawn_derivative_check_regional_predator = yes
	}
}

	remove_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_broken_staff
}

# COUNTRY scope. Provider-owned public ideas, route state, and institutional
# identity receive a proof-gated lifecycle callback before shared teardown.
infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_provider_cleanup = {
	clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved
	}
	else = {
		infantry_spawn_derivative_find_cleanup_registry_row = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
				has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_public_package_proved
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
				check_variable = {
					var = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id
					value = constant:chaos_unit_family_registry.minimum_provider_id
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_registry_index > constant:infantry_spawn_event.invalid_index }
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_current_registry_provider_id value = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id compare = equals }
			}
			meta_effect = {
				text = { chaos_unit_family_provider_[PROVIDER]_event19_cleanup_derivative = yes }
				PROVIDER = "[?infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_id|.0]"
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved } }
		infantry_spawn_mark_ledger_invariant_failure = yes
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_handle_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_infantry_spawn_derivative_country = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeat_recorded }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.defeat }
		infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_provider_cleanup = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_commit_defeat_after_provider_cleanup = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.none }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_commit_defeat_after_provider_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_infantry_spawn_derivative_country = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeat_recorded }
		}
		infantry_spawn_achievement_record_current_derivative_defeat = yes
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeat_recorded
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeated
		infantry_spawn_record_derivative_defeat_history = yes
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_active
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_decisions_open
		clr_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_opening_crisis_active
		infantry_spawn_derivative_cancel_district_integration = yes
		infantry_spawn_derivative_cancel_submission_warning = yes
		remove_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_integrate_conquered_district_mission
		remove_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_submission_warning_mission
		remove_mission = infantry_spawn_derivative_survive_former_parent_front
		infantry_spawn_derivative_remove_active_ideas = yes
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_hunted_remnant
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_scattered_enclaves
		add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_encircled_remnant
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_claimant_broken_staff
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_devouring_fragments
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_fading_host
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
			add_ideas = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_crumbling_host
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeat_report_dispatched } }
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_defeat_report_dispatched
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_claimant_breakaway }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.917 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_zombie_derivative }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.911 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_ghost_derivative }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.913 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_golem_derivative }
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr19.915 }
			}
		}
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_lock_and_remove_tracked_formations = {
	set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_cleanup_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	infantry_spawn_prepare_annex_cleanup_unit_set = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_unit_preflight > constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
		infantry_spawn_lock_annexed_country_templates = yes
		infantry_spawn_delete_annex_cleanup_unit_set = yes
		infantry_spawn_prove_annex_cleanup_unit_set_absent = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_units = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
			infantry_spawn_delete_annexed_country_templates = yes
			infantry_spawn_prove_annexed_country_templates_absent = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_remaining_templates = constant:infantry_spawn_event.first_index } }
				set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_cleanup_succeeded = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.one }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_cleanup_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_division_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
		set_variable = { infantry_spawn_active_lot_count = constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero }
	}
}

infantry_spawn_derivative_clear_owned_state_markers = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
			limit = {
				has_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
				check_variable = { var = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid value = ROOT.infantry_spawn_derivative_package_owner_uid compare = equals }
			}
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_muster_depot
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_sustainment_site
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integration_underway
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_integrated_district
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_muster_district
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_zombie_population_pressure
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_recognized_anchor
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_golem_bound_district
			clr_state_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_ghost_population_pressure
			clear_variable = infantry_spawn_derivative_site_owner_uid
		}
	}
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_derivative_owned_marker_states
	clear_array = infantry_spawn_derivative_diplomacy_target_countries
}

# Annex cleanup is proof-gated. Exact UID/cohort cleanup may delete a subset
# before an invariant failure, so every private ledger and identity flag remains
# intact until both the defeated country and annexer prove the tracked set absent.
infantry_spawn_derivative_final_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_infantry_spawn_derivative_country = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_country_cleanup_complete }
		}
		set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_defeat_cleanup_in_progress
		infantry_spawn_derivative_lock_and_remove_tracked_formations = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { infantry_spawn_family_cleanup_succeeded > constant:infantry_spawn_derivative_package_value.zero } }
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.final }
			infantry_spawn_derivative_dispatch_provider_cleanup = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = infantry_spawn_derivative_provider_cleanup_proved }
				infantry_spawn_derivative_finalize_cleanup_after_formation_proof = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
				infantry_spawn_enqueue_annex_cleanup_retry = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { infantry_spawn_derivative_cleanup_phase = constant:chaos_unit_family_derivative_cleanup_phase.none }
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = infantry_spawn_annex_cleanup_invariant_failure
			infantry_spawn_enqueue_annex_cleanup_retry = yes
		}
	}
}
